is the time step size, and
k
is an
integer. The quadrature weights
\left\{\tilde{w}_{l}\right\}_{l=0}^{1}

over a single quadrature interval are

\tilde{w}_{l=0,1}=\frac{1}{2}.

(286)

The error of the numerical integration over a single interval
is third order in
\Delta t
.

Recall that
\hat{U}\left(t,t^{\prime}\right)
, being a time evolution
operator, satisfies

\hat{U}\left(t,t^{\prime}\right)=\hat{U}\left(t,t^{\prime\prime}\right)\hat{U}\left(t^{\prime\prime},t^{\prime}\right).

(287)

This allows us to rewrite, for instance,
\hat{U}\left(t,0\right)

as

\hat{U}\left(t_{n},0\right)=\prod_{k=0}^{n-1}\left[\hat{U}\left(t_{n-k},t_{n-k-1}\right)\right],

(288)

where we have introduced the notation

\prod_{l=l_{i}}^{l_{f}}\left\{x_{l_{\vphantom{i}}}\right\}=x_{l_{i}}x_{l_{i}+1}\cdots x_{l_{f}-1}x_{l_{f}}.

(289)

Next, let us approximate
\hat{U}\left(t_{n-k},t_{n-k-1}\right)

using the aforementioned quadrature scheme:

\displaystyle\hat{U}\left(t_{n-k},t_{n-k-1}\right)

\displaystyle=T\left\{e^{-i\int_{t_{n-k-1}}^{t_{n-k}}dt^{\prime\prime}\hat{H}\left(t^{\prime\prime}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(-i\right)^{M}}{M!}\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left\{\int_{t_{n-k-1}}^{t_{n-k}}dt^{\left(m\right)}\right\}T\left\{\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left[\hat{H}\left(t^{\left(m\right)}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left(-i\right)^{M}}{M!}\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left\{\sum_{l_{m}=0}^{1}\left[\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l_{m}}\right]\right\}T\left\{\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left[\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l_{m}}\right)\right]\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right].

(290)

We note that we can rewrite the following term in Eq. (
290
)
as

\displaystyle\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left\{\sum_{l_{m}=0}^{1}\left[\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l_{m}}\right]\right\}T\left\{\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left[\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l_{m}}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\sum_{M_{n-k-l}=0}^{M}\right\}\delta_{M_{n-k}+M_{n-k-1},M}\left[\frac{M!}{\left(M_{n-k}\right)!\left(M_{n-k-1}\right)!}\right]\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\left[\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)\right]^{M_{n-k-l}}\right\}.

(291)

where
\delta_{i,j}
is the kronecker delta function. Substituting
Eq. (
291
) into (
290
)
yields

\displaystyle\hat{U}\left(t_{n-k},t_{n-k-1}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left\{-i\Delta t\right\}^{M}}{M!}\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left\{\sum_{l_{m}=0}^{1}\left[\tilde{w}_{l_{m}}\right]\right\}T\left\{\prod_{m=1}^{M}\left[\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)\right]\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left\{-i\Delta t\right\}^{M}}{\cancel{M!}}\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\sum_{M_{n-k-l}=0}^{M}\right\}\delta_{M_{n-k}+M_{n-k-1},M}\left[\frac{M!}{\left(M_{n-k}\right)!\left(M_{n-k-1}\right)!}\right]

\displaystyle\phantom{\mathrel{=}\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left\{-i\Delta t\right\}^{M}}{\cancel{M!}}\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\sum_{M_{n-k-l}=0}^{M}\right\}}\mathord{\times}\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\left[\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)\right]^{M_{n-k-l}}\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{M=0}^{\infty}\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\sum_{M_{n-k-l}=0}^{M}\right\}\delta_{M_{n-k}+M_{n-k-1},M}\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\frac{\left[-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)\right]^{M_{n-k-l}}}{\left(M_{n-k-l}\right)!}\right\}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathord{+}\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{\sum_{M_{n-k-l}=0}^{M}\frac{\left[-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)\right]^{M_{n-k-l}}}{\left(M_{n-k-l}\right)!}\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left\{e^{-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)}\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right].

(292)

Using Eq. (
292
),
we can write

\displaystyle\hat{U}\left(t_{n},0\right)

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=0}^{n-1}\left[\hat{U}_{I}\left(t_{n-k},t_{n-k-1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\{\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left[e^{-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)}\right]+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\{\prod_{l=0}^{1}\left[e^{-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{l}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k-l}\right)}\right]\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=0}^{n}\left\{e^{-i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}\left(t_{n-k}\right)}\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right],

(293)

where
w_{n;k}
are the weights for the composite quadrature
scheme:

w_{n;k}=\begin{cases}0,&\text{if }k=-1,n+1,\\
\frac{1}{2},&\text{if }k=0,n,\\
1,&\text{if }0<k<n.\end{cases}

(294)

with the
k=-1,n+1
cases added for later convenience.
Similarly, we have

\hat{U}\left(0,t_{n}\right)=\prod_{k=0}^{n}\left\{e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}\left(t_{k}\right)}\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right].

(295)

Next, we rewrite the total Hamiltonian
\hat{H}\left(t\right)
as

\hat{H}\left(t\right)=\sum_{\nu\in\left\{x,z\right\}}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t\right)+\sum_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t\right),

(296)

where

\hat{H}_{\nu\in\left\{x,z\right\}}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t\right)=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\hat{H}_{u;\nu}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t\right),

(297)

\hat{H}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}^{\left(AB\right)}\left(t\right)=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\hat{H}_{u;\nu}^{\left(AB\right)}\left(t\right),

(298)

\hat{H}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}^{\left(B\right)}=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\hat{H}_{u;\nu}^{\left(B\right)},

(299)

\hat{H}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t\right)=\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(AB\right)}\left(t\right)+\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)},

(300)

and
\hat{H}_{u;x}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t\right)
,
\hat{H}_{u;z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t\right)
,

\hat{H}_{u;\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}^{\left(B\right)}
, and
\hat{H}_{u;\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}^{\left(AB\right)}

are defined by Eqs. (
4
), (
5
),
(
7
), and (
9
).
Using

e^{\Delta t\left\{\hat{X}+\hat{Y}\right\}}=e^{\frac{\Delta t}{2}\hat{X}}e^{\Delta t\hat{Y}}e^{\frac{\Delta t}{2}\hat{X}}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right],

(301)

Eq. (
300
)
we rewrite
e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}\left(t_{k}\right)}
as

\displaystyle e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}\left(t_{k}\right)}

\displaystyle=e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\left\{\left[\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)+\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\right]+\left[\hat{H}_{x}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)+\hat{H}_{y}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\right]\right\}}

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\left\{\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)+\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\left\{\hat{H}_{x}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)+\hat{H}_{y}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\left\{\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)+\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\right\}}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{y}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{x}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{y}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{3}\right],

(302)

Next, we substitute Eq. (
302
)
into Eq. (
295
),
and insert various resolutions of the identity:

\displaystyle\hat{U}\left(0,t_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=0}^{n}\left\{e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}\left(t_{k}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle=\prod_{k=0}^{n}\left\{\hat{1}_{z;-1;2k}^{\left(\sigma\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=\prod_{k=1}^{n}}\left.\quad\mathord{\times}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{y}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}\hat{1}_{y;-1;2k}^{\left(\sigma\right)}e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{x}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}\hat{1}_{y;-1;2k+1}^{\left(\sigma\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{y}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=\prod_{k=1}^{n}}\left.\quad\mathord{\times}\hat{1}_{z;-1;2k+1}^{\left(\sigma\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta tw_{n;k}\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)}\right\}+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right],

(303)

where

\hat{1}_{\nu;\alpha;k}^{\left(\sigma\right)}=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;k}=\pm 1}\right\}\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;k}\nu\right\rangle\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;k}\nu\right|;

(304)

\hat{\sigma}_{\nu;r}\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;k}\nu\right\rangle=\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha;k}\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;k}\nu\right\rangle,

(305)

with

\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;k}=\left(\sigma_{\nu;r=-NL;\alpha;k},\ldots,\sigma_{\nu;r=\left(N+1\right)L-1;\alpha;k}\right).

(306)

Expanding the resolutions of the identity yields:

\displaystyle\hat{U}\left(0,t_{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left[\prod_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{y;r+uL;-1;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\prod_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;-1;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{l=1}^{n}\left\{\delta_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;-1;2l-1},\sigma_{z;r+uL;-1;2l}}\right\}e^{i\tilde{\phi}_{-1;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)}\tilde{I}_{-1;n}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{y;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\tilde{I}_{-1;n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)e^{i\Delta t\sum_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu;k=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;-1;q}\right)}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}z\right\rangle\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;n+1}z\right|+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right],

(307)

where

\tilde{w}_{n;q}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{4},&\text{if }q=0,1,2n,2n+1,\\
\frac{1}{2},&\text{if }2\leq q\leq 2n-1;\end{cases}

(308)

\tilde{\phi}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\tilde{\phi}_{\alpha;n;q}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right),

(309)

with

\tilde{\phi}_{\alpha;n;q}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right)=-\frac{\alpha\Delta t}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q}H_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor\Delta t;\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right),

(310)

\displaystyle H_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t;\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}z\right|\hat{H}_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t\right)\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}h_{z;r}\left(t\right)\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}\mathop{+}\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}J_{z,z;r,r+1}\left(t\right)\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}\sigma_{z;r+uL+1;\alpha;q};

(311)

\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{y;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=\prod_{l=0}^{n}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\alpha;r+uL;n;k=l}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r+uL;\alpha;2l},\sigma_{y;r+uL;\alpha;2l+1}\right)\right\},

(312)

with

\tilde{I}_{\alpha;r;n;k}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;2l},\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;2l+1}\right)=\begin{cases}\left\langle\sigma_{y;r;-1;2l}y\right|e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}h_{x;r}\left(t_{k}\right)\hat{\sigma}_{x;r}}\left|\sigma_{y;r;-1;2l+1}y\right\rangle,&\text{if }\alpha=-1,\\
\left\langle\sigma_{y;r;1;2l+1}y\right|e^{-i\Delta tw_{n;k}h_{x;r}\left(t_{k}\right)\hat{\sigma}_{x;r}}\left|\sigma_{y;r;1;2l}y\right\rangle,&\text{if }\alpha=1;\end{cases}

(313)

h_{x;r+uL}\left(t_{k}\right)=h_{x;r}\left(t_{k}\right),

(314)

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\nu;k=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha,q}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;q}\nu\right|\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha;q}\nu\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}\left[\mathcal{E}_{v;r}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\left(t_{k}\right)\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;k}\left\{\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}+\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\phantom{=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\right],

(315)

with

\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}=\mathcal{E}_{v;r}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\left(t=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor\Delta t\right);

(316)

\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r+uL;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\right\},

(317)

with

\displaystyle\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\begin{cases}\prod_{l=0}^{n}\left\{I^{\left(y\to z\right)}\left(\sigma_{z;r;-1;2l};\sigma_{y;r;-1;2l}\right)I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;-1;2l+1};\sigma_{z;r;-1;2l+1}\right)\right\},&\text{if }\alpha=-1,\\
\prod_{k=0}^{n}\left\{I^{\left(y\to z\right)}\left(\sigma_{z;r;1;2l+1};\sigma_{y;r;1;2l+1}\right)I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1;2l};\sigma_{z;r;1;2l}\right)\right\},&\text{if }\alpha=1,\end{cases}

(318)

and

\displaystyle I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}},\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\equiv\left.\left\langle\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}y\right|\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left\{\frac{\left(1+\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right)}{2}-i\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}\frac{\left(1-\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right)}{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\left\{1-i\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}+\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}+i\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right\},

(319)

\displaystyle I^{\left(y\to z\right)}\left(\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}},\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\{I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}},\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right)\right\}^{*}.

(320)

Note that the labels “lcafc” and “tfc” that appear
in Eq. (
307
) are
initialisms for “longitudinal coupler and field components” and
“transverse field components” respectively.

Next, we rewrite the term
\hat{H}_{\nu;k}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha,q}\right)

in Eq. (
307
) as:

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\nu;k=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha,q}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}\left[\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;q}\left\{\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}+\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}}\left.\mathop{+}\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\right],

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}\left[\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\left\{\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}+\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;q}\right\}\vphantom{-\frac{\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\right\}^{2}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}}\left.\quad\mathop{\times}\left\{\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}+\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;q}\right\}-\frac{\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\right\}^{2}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;q}\right)\right\}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha;q}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}\frac{\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\right\}^{2}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}},

(321)

where
\hat{H}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}^{\left(B\right)}

is given by Eq. (
299
),
and
\hat{D}_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(z\right)

is the displacement operator
(
Ballentine 2014
)
:

\hat{D}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(z\right)=e^{z\hat{b}_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{\dagger}-z^{*}\hat{b}_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}}.

(322)

Next, we use the fact that the displacement operator
\hat{D}_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(z\right)

is unitary to write:

\displaystyle e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu;k=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;-1;q}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad=e^{-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\frac{\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\right\}^{2}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right)\right\}e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right)\right\},

(323)

where

\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}f\left(u,r,\epsilon,\ldots\right)=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\epsilon}f\left(u,r,\epsilon,\ldots\right),

(324)

with
f\left(u,r,\epsilon,\ldots\right)
being an arbitrary
function. Using the Baker–Campbell–Hausdorff
formula:

e^{\hat{X}}e^{\hat{Y}}=e^{\hat{Z}},

(325)

where

\hat{Z}=\hat{X}+\hat{Y}+\frac{1}{2}\left[\hat{X},\hat{Y}\right]+\frac{1}{12}\left[\hat{X},\left[\hat{X},\hat{Y}\right]\right]-\ldots,

(326)

and the fact that

\left[\hat{b}_{\nu_{1};r_{1};\epsilon_{1}}^{\vphantom{\dagger}},\hat{b}_{\nu_{2};r_{2};\epsilon_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]=\delta_{\nu_{1},\nu_{2}}\delta_{r_{1},r_{2}}\delta_{\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2}}\hat{1}_{\nu_{1};r_{1};\epsilon_{1}}^{\left(\mathcal{B}\right)}\hat{1}_{\nu_{2};r_{2};\epsilon_{2}}^{\left(\mathcal{B}\right)},

(327)

we can write

\displaystyle\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}e^{-\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}},

(328)

\displaystyle\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}}e^{-\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\hat{b}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}}.

(329)

We can go further and insert a coherent state resolution
of the identity [see Eq. (
281
)
in Appendix
A
]:

\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;\alpha;q}\,e^{-\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;\alpha;q}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;\alpha;q}}\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;\alpha;q}\right\rangle\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;\alpha;q}\right|=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{1}_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{\left(\mathcal{B}\right)}\right\},\quad\text{with }j\in\left\{0,1\right\},

(330)

in between the second and third exponential terms in both
Eq. (
328
) and (
329
)
to get

\displaystyle\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\vphantom{\dagger}}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;\alpha;q}\,e^{-\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;\alpha;q}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;\alpha;q}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{-\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\left\{b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;q}^{\vphantom{*}}-b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;q}^{*}\right\}}\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;\alpha;q}\right\rangle\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;\alpha;q}\right|,

(331)

\displaystyle\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\hat{D}_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}^{\dagger}\left(\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;\alpha;q}\,e^{-\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;\alpha;q}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;\alpha;q}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\left\{b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;q}^{\vphantom{*}}-b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;q}^{*}\right\}}\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;\alpha;q}\right\rangle\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;\alpha;q}\right|,

(332)

where the set of labels
\left\{j;\alpha;q\right\}
is
used to distinguish between the different instances of the resolution
of the identity. Applying Eqs. (
331
)
and (
332
) to Eq. (
323
)
yields:

\displaystyle e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu;k=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;-1;q}\right)}

\displaystyle=\prod_{j=0,1}\left\{\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;-1;q}\,e^{-\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;-1;q}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;-1;q}}\right\}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\frac{\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\right\}^{2}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\left[b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;q}^{\vphantom{*}}-b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;q}^{*}\right]}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{-\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\left[b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;q}^{\vphantom{*}}-b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;q}^{*}\right]}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;-1;q}\right|e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)}}\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;-1;q}\right\rangle\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;-1;q}\right\rangle\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;-1;q}\right|.

(333)

To calculate the matrix elements of
e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)}}
,
we use the following identity

f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)\equiv\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|a^{\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle=e^{b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}a},

(334)

where
\left|b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right\rangle
and
\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle

are coherent states and
a
is a complex number. Equation (
334
)
can be proven from the following operator relation:

g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}\right)\hat{b}=\hat{b}g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}-\hat{1}\right),

(335)

where
g
is an arbitrary function. Equation (
335
)
can be proven by acting both sides of Eq. (
335
)
by an arbitrary occupation number state
\left|n\right\rangle
:

\displaystyle g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}\right)\hat{b}\left|n\right\rangle=\hat{b}g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}-\hat{1}\right)\left|n\right\rangle

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}\right)\left\{\hat{b}\left|n\right\rangle\right\}=\hat{b}\left\{g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}-\hat{1}\right)\left|n\right\rangle\right\}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}\right)\left\{\sqrt{n}\left|n-1\right\rangle\right\}=\hat{b}\left\{g\left(n-1\right)\left|n\right\rangle\right\}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left\{g\left(\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}\right)\left|n-1\right\rangle\right\}=g\left(n-1\right)\left\{\hat{b}\left|n\right\rangle\right\}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left\{g\left(n-1\right)\left|n-1\right\rangle\right\}=g\left(n-1\right)\left\{\sqrt{n}\left|n-1\right\rangle\right\}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}g\left(n-1\right)\left|n-1\right\rangle=\sqrt{n}g\left(n-1\right)\left|n-1\right\rangle.

(336)

Since the set of occupation number states form a complete
basis, Eq. (
336
)
implies that Eq. (
335
)
is valid for all states in the single-boson Hilbert space. By differentiating

f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)
with respect
to
a
, and using Eqs. (
276
),
(
277
), and (
335
),
one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\left\{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\left\{a^{\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}}\right\}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}a^{\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}-\hat{1}}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|\hat{b}^{\dagger}a^{\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}}\hat{b}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|a^{\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right).

(337)

We can then solve the ordinary differential equation as
follows:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\left\{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)\right\}=b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\frac{1}{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)}\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\left\{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)\right\}=b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{a}da^{\prime}\,\left[\frac{1}{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)}\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\left\{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)\right\}\right]=\int_{1}^{a}da^{\prime}\,b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{a}\left[\left(da^{\prime}\,\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\left\{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)\right\}\right)\frac{1}{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)}\right]=\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(1\right)}^{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)}\left[df\,\frac{1}{f}\right]=\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle\ln\left(\frac{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)}{f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(1\right)}\right)=\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)=f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(1\right)e^{\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)=\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|1^{\hat{b}^{\dagger}\hat{b}}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle e^{\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)=\left\langle b^{\vphantom{\prime}}\right|\hat{1}\left|b^{\prime}\right\rangle e^{\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)=e^{b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}e^{\left(a-1\right)b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow

\displaystyle f_{b^{\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}}\left(a\right)=e^{b^{*\vphantom{\prime}}b^{\prime}a},

(338)

where we used Eq. (
280
)
in going from the third last to the second last line. This completes
the proof of Eq. (
334
).
Using Eqs. (
299
) and
(
334
), we can express
the matrix elements of
e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)}}

as

\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;-1;q}\right|e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu}^{\left(B\right)}}\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;-1;q}\right\rangle=e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;k}^{*}b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;k}^{\vphantom{*}}e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}}.

(339)

Substituting Eq. (
339
)
into Eq. (
333
)
yields

\displaystyle e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\hat{H}_{\nu;k=\left\lfloor q/2\right\rfloor}^{\left(AB+B\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;-1;q}\right)}

\displaystyle=\prod_{j=0,1}\left\{\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;-1;q}\,e^{-\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;-1;q}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{\nu;j;-1;q}}\right\}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\frac{\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\right\}^{2}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\left[b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;q}^{\vphantom{*}}-b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;q}^{*}\right]}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{-\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;-1;q}\right\}\left[b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;q}^{\vphantom{*}}-b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;q}^{*}\right]}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;0;-1;k}^{*}b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;1;-1;k}^{\vphantom{*}}e^{i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left|\mathbf{b}_{\nu;0;-1;q}\right\rangle\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{\nu;1;-1;q}\right|.

(340)

Next, we return to Eq. (
333
)
and rewrite it as follows:

\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)=\text{Tr}^{\left(B\right)}\left\{\hat{U}\left(t_{n},0\right)\hat{\rho}^{\left(i\right)}\hat{U}\left(0,t_{n}\right)\right\}=\text{Tr}^{\left(B\right)}\left\{\hat{U}^{\dagger}\left(0,t_{n}\right)\hat{\rho}^{\left(i\right)}\hat{U}\left(0,t_{n}\right)\right\}.

(341)

Next, we evaluate the trace
\text{Tr}^{\left(B\right)}\left\{\cdots\right\}

using the coherent states
\left|\mathbf{b}_{y;0;0};\mathbf{b}_{z;0;0}\right\rangle

as basis states [see Eq. (
283
)
for expression for trace in coherent state basis]:

\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)=\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{y;0;0}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{b}_{z;0;0}\,e^{-\mathbf{b}_{y;0;0}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{y;0;0}-\mathbf{b}_{z;0;0}\cdot\mathbf{b}_{z;0;0}}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{y;0;0};\mathbf{b}_{z;0;0}\right|\hat{U}^{\dagger}\left(0,t_{n}\right)\hat{\rho}^{\left(i\right)}\hat{U}\left(0,t_{n}\right)\left|\mathbf{b}_{y;0;0};\mathbf{b}_{z;0;0}\right\rangle,

(342)

Next, using Eqs. (
13
)-(
16
),
(
17
),(
20
),
(
23
), (
280
),
(
307
), (
334
),
(

),
and (

) we get:

\displaystyle\rho^{\left(A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;2n+1},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;2n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\equiv\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;2n+1}z\right|\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;2n+1}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left[\prod_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{y;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\prod_{q=0}^{2n}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{l=1}^{n}\left\{\delta_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;2l-1},\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;2l}}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{i\sum_{\alpha=\pm 1}\tilde{\phi}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)}e^{2\sum_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{y;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\int\mathcal{D}b\,F_{n\vphantom{\nu;r;\epsilon}}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};b\right)\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\left\{\left[\mathcal{Z}_{u;y}^{\left(B\right)}\mathcal{Z}_{u;z}^{\left(B\right)}\right]^{-1}\right\}\rho^{\left(i,A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right],

(343)

where

\rho^{\left(i,A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}\right)=\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0}z\right|\hat{\rho}^{\left(i,A\right)}\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}z\right\rangle=\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0}z\vphantom{\Psi^{\left(i,A\right)}}\right.\left|\Psi^{\left(i,A\right)}\right\rangle\left\langle\Psi^{\left(i,A\right)}\right|\left.\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}z\vphantom{\Psi^{\left(i,A\right)}}\right\rangle;

(344)

\int\mathcal{D}b
is a bosonic measure:

\int\mathcal{D}b=\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{\int\mathcal{D}b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}\right\},

(345)

with

\int\mathcal{D}b_{\nu;r;\epsilon}=\int d^{2}b_{\nu;r;\epsilon;j=0;\alpha=0}\,\prod_{q=0}^{2n+1}\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{j=0,1}\left\{\int d^{2}b_{\nu;r;\epsilon;j;\alpha;q}\right\};

(346)

F_{n\vphantom{\nu;r;\epsilon}}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};b\right)

is an integrand that is Gaussian in the bosonic fields with:

\displaystyle F_{n\vphantom{\nu;r;\epsilon}}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};b\right)

\displaystyle=\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}\left\{F_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;n}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};\right.\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}F_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;n}}\left.\left.\quad b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;j=0,1;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};\right.\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{\epsilon}F_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;n}}\left.\quad\vphantom{F_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;n}}\left.b_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon;j=0;\alpha=0}\right)\right\},

(347)

\displaystyle F_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};b_{\nu;r;\epsilon;j=0,1;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};b_{\nu;r;\epsilon;j=0;\alpha=0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=e^{-\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}+\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\psi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}},

(348)

\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}=\begin{pmatrix}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0}\\
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=1}\end{pmatrix},

(349)

\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0}=\begin{pmatrix}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=1}\\
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=0}\\
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=-1}\end{pmatrix},

(350)

\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=1}=\begin{pmatrix}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=1}\\
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=-1}\end{pmatrix},

(351)

\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=\pm 1}=\begin{pmatrix}b_{\nu;r;\epsilon;j;\alpha;q=0}\\
\vdots\\
b_{\nu;r;\epsilon;j;\alpha;q=2n+1}\end{pmatrix},

(352)

\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0;\alpha=0}=\begin{pmatrix}b_{\nu;r;\epsilon}\end{pmatrix},

(353)

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}=\begin{pmatrix}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0}\\
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=1}\end{pmatrix},

(354)

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0}=\begin{pmatrix}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=1}\\
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=0}\\
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=-1}\end{pmatrix},

(355)

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=1}=\begin{pmatrix}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=1}\\
\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j;\alpha=-1}\end{pmatrix},

(356)

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0,1;\alpha=\pm 1}=-\alpha\left(-1\right)^{j}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\begin{pmatrix}\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha;q=0}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q=0}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\\
\vdots\\
\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha;q=2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q=2n+1}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\end{pmatrix},

(357)

\lambda_{\nu;r+uL;\epsilon}=\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon},

(358)

\mathcal{E}_{v;r+uL;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}=\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)},

(359)

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;j=0;\alpha=0}=\begin{pmatrix}0\end{pmatrix},

(360)

\boldsymbol{\psi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}=-\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n},

(361)

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}\\
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\end{pmatrix},

(362)

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,1}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,1}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,-1}\\
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,1}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(1\right)}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(1\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(1\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\end{pmatrix},

(363)

\mathbf{1}_{\left(N\right)\times\left(N\right)}=\underbrace{\begin{pmatrix}1&0&\cdots&\cdots&0\\
0&1&\ddots&&\vdots\\
\vdots&\ddots&\ddots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\vdots&&\ddots&1&0\\
0&\cdots&\cdots&0&1\end{pmatrix}}_{N\text{ columns}}\left.\vphantom{\begin{pmatrix}1\\
0\\
\vdots\\
\vdots\\
0\end{pmatrix}}\right\}N\text{ rows},

(364)

\mathbf{0}_{\left(N_{1}\right)\times\left(N_{1}\right)}=\underbrace{\begin{pmatrix}0&\cdots&\cdots&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&&&&\vdots\\
\vdots&&&&\vdots\\
\vdots&&&&\vdots\\
0&\cdots&\cdots&\cdots&0\end{pmatrix}}_{N_{2}\text{ columns}}\left.\vphantom{\begin{pmatrix}1\\
0\\
\vdots\\
\vdots\\
0\end{pmatrix}}\right\}N_{1}\text{ rows},

(365)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon},

(366)

\delta_{q,q^{\prime}}=\begin{cases}1,&\text{if }q=q^{\prime},\\
0,&\text{if }q\neq q^{\prime},\end{cases}

(367)

g_{\nu;\epsilon}=e^{-\beta\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}},

(368)

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(369)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1},

(370)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}},

(371)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}},

(372)

f_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;\alpha;q}=e^{-\alpha i\Delta t\tilde{w}_{n;q}\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}},

(373)

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(1\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(374)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}},

(375)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},2n+1},

(376)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}},

(377)

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\end{pmatrix}.

(378)

The product of Gaussian integrals in Eq. (
343
)
can be evaluated using the identity
(
Altland and Simons 2010
)
:

\mathcal{D}b_{\nu;r;\epsilon}e^{-\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}+\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\psi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}}=\frac{e^{\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\psi}}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}}}{\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right]},

(379)

where the calculations of
\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}

and
\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right]
are very long
and cumbersome. The matrix
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}
was deliberately
cast in the above block form such that we could exploit certain block
matrix identities. Let
\mathbf{M}
be a block matrix of the form

\mathbf{M}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{M}_{1,1}&\mathbf{M}_{1,2}\\
\mathbf{M}_{2,1}&\mathbf{M}_{2,2,}\end{pmatrix}.

(380)

Assuming that
\mathbf{M}_{1,1}
is invertible, then we
have the following identities:

\det\left[\mathbf{M}\right]=\det\left[\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right]\det\left[\mathbf{M}_{2,2}-\mathbf{M}_{2,1}\left(\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{1,2}\right],

(381)

\mathbf{M}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{M}_{1,1}&\mathbf{M}_{1,2}\\
\mathbf{M}_{2,1}&\mathbf{M}_{2,2,}\end{pmatrix}.

(382)

\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{1,1}=\left(\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{1,2}\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{2,1},

(383)

\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{1,2}=-\left(\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{1,2}\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{2,2},

(384)

\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{2,1}=-\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{2,2}\mathbf{M}_{2,1}\left(\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right)^{-1},

(385)

\left[\mathbf{M}^{-1}\right]_{2,2}=\left(\mathbf{M}_{2,2}-\mathbf{M}_{2,1}\left(\mathbf{M}_{1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{M}_{1,2}\right)^{-1}.

(386)

In what follows, we use Eqs. (
381
)-(
386
)
to calculate
\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}
and

\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right]
. If we let

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}=\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1},

(387)

then according to Eqs. (
382
)(
386
),
we can write

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}\\
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\end{pmatrix},

(388)

with

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}=\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0},

(389)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}=-\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1},

(390)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}=-\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1},

(391)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}=\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\right)^{-1},

(392)

\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}=\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}.

(393)

Let us first calculate
\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}
.
To calculate
\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}
,
we recast
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}
as a
2\times 2
block
matrix so that Eqs. (
381
)-(
386
)
are applicable:

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+3\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\end{pmatrix},

(394)

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,1}^{\prime}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\end{pmatrix}.

(395)

Because
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}
in Eq. (
394
)
has a simple
2\times 2
block matrix structure, it is straightforward
to see from Eqs. (
383
)-(
386
)
that

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+3\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}&-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(1\right)}&-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(1\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(1\right)}&\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\end{pmatrix}.

(396)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}
.
Using Eqs. (
366
),
(
374
), (
375
),
and (
396
)
we get

\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(1\right)}&-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(397)

with

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;0}g_{\nu;\epsilon}.

(398)

Next, we calculate
\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}
.
Using Eqs. (
366
),
(
369
), (
372
),
and (
396
)
we get

\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}&-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\\
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}&\mathbf{0}_{\left(1\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}\\
\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(399)

with

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;0}g_{\nu;\epsilon}.

(400)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}
.
Using Eqs. (
366
),
(
370
)-(
372
),
(
374
)-(
377
),
and (
399
)
we get

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}

\displaystyle\quad=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}&-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\\
\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}&\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(401)

with

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}},

(402)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon},

(403)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}},

(404)

\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{2}}.

(405)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}
.
Using Eqs. (
378
),
(
393
), and (
401
)-(
405
)
we get

\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}&\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}&\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(406)

with

\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}-\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}},

(407)

\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon},

(408)

\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=-\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}},

(409)

\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}-\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{2}}.

(410)

Now that we have calculated
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}
,
we need to calculate its inverse, i.e.
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}

[see Eq. (
392
)].
Using Eqs. (
382
)-(
386
),
and (
392
),
we can write

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(411)

with

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}=\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}-\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1},

(412)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}=-\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1},

(413)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}=-\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1},

(414)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}=\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}-\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right)^{-1}.

(415)

Let us first calculate
\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}
.
For an invertible matrix
\mathbf{M}
, we can calculate its inverse
using the following formula:

\mathbf{M}^{-1}=\frac{1}{\det\left[\mathbf{M}\right]}\left(\mathbf{q}\right)^{\text{T}},

(416)

where
\mathbf{q}
is the cofactor matrix:

\left[\mathbf{q}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\left(-1\right)^{q_{1}+q_{2}}\left[\mathbf{D}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}},

(417)

with
\left[\mathbf{D}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}
being the
determinant of the matrix that results from deleting row
q_{1}

and column
q_{2}
of
\mathbf{M}
. By inspection, we applied the
above formula to
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}
and obtained

\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}.

(418)

with

\Theta_{q,q^{\prime}}=\begin{cases}1,&\text{if }k\geq k^{\prime},\\
0,&\text{if }k<k^{\prime}.\end{cases}

(419)

The most straightforward way for the reader to convince
themselves of this is to simply evaluate the matrix multiplication
of
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}
with its inverse using
Eqs. (
407
) and
(
418
):

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}-\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}\right)\left(\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}+\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{-}\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}+1}\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}-\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}+1}\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\hphantom{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{-}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}-\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\hphantom{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{-}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{1}}-\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}-\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}.

(420)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}
.
Using Eqs. (
409
)
and (
418
)
we get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q_{3}}\left(-\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{2n+1,q_{3}}\right)\left(\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=-\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}}\right).

(421)

Next, we calculate
\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}
.
Using Eqs. (
408
)
and (
418
)
we get

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{q_{3}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{3}+1}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\left(-\delta_{q_{3},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle=-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\delta_{0,q_{2}}.

(422)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}
.
Using Eqs. (
409
)
and (
422
)
we get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\left(-\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{2n+1,q_{3}}\right)\left(-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left(\delta_{q_{3},0}+\Theta_{q_{3},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\delta_{0,q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{0,q_{2}}.

(423)

Next, we calculate
\left(\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right)^{-1}
.
Using Eqs. (
410
),
(
415
), and
(
423
)
we get

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}-\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}-\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{2}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{0,q_{2}}.

(424)

To calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}
,
we calculate the inverse of
\left(\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right)^{-1}

as expressed in Eq. (
424
).
We use Eqs. (
416
) and
(
417
) to calculate the inverse:

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(425)

Let us verify this result:

\displaystyle\left[\left(\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}-\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{3}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{3}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{0,q_{3}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\sum_{q_{3}}}\quad\times\left(\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{3}+1}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{+}\cancel{\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}}-\cancel{\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{2}}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\Theta_{2n+1,q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\cancel{\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+2}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}}-\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\Theta_{q_{2},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\left\{\Theta_{2n+1,q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{2n+1,q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\left\{\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\phantom{\quad\mathord{-}}\left.\quad\mathord{+}\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\Theta_{q_{2},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\cancel{\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\left\{\Theta_{2n+1,q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{2n+1,q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)}\left.\quad\mathord{-}\left\{\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\cancel{\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}.

(426)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}
.
Using Eqs. (
413
),
(
422
),
and (
425
) we
get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\delta_{0,q_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\sum_{q_{3}}}\quad\mathord{\times}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{3}+1}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\mathord{\times}\left(\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{0,q_{3}}\delta_{q_{3},q_{2}}+\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{0,q_{3}}\Theta_{q_{3},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\mathord{\times}}\left.\quad\mathord{+}\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{0,q_{3}}\Theta_{q_{2},q_{3}+1}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\left(\delta_{0,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{2},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(427)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}
.
Using Eqs. (
414
),
(
421
),
and (
425
) we
get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{3}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{3},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\sum_{q_{3}}}\quad\mathop{\times}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}}\right)\delta_{q_{3},2n+1}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\quad\mathop{\times}\left(\sum_{q_{3}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{3}}\delta_{q_{3},2n+1}+\sum_{q_{3}}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{3}+1}\delta_{q_{3},2n+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\quad\mathop{\phantom{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\sum_{q_{3}}\Theta_{q_{3},q_{1}+1}\delta_{q_{3},2n+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{3}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\quad\mathop{\times}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(428)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}
.
Using Eqs. (
412
),
(
418
),
(
422
),
and (
425
) we
get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{3}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\delta_{0,q_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{3}}}\quad\mathop{\times}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{3}+1}\prod_{q=q_{3}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\delta_{q_{3},2n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{3}}}\quad\mathop{\times}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}}\quad\mathop{\times}\left(\Theta_{2n+1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{2n+1,q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(1+\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\left(1+\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(429)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}
. This is done
in several steps: first we cast
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}

in block form, and then we calculate those elements of the block matrix
that we ultimately require in order to calculate the right-hand-side
of Eq. (
379
). Using Eqs. (
390
),
(
399
),
and (
411
)
we get

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,-1}\\
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(430)

with

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1},

(431)

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1},

(432)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}=-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1},

(433)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,-1}=-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;0,1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1},

(434)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}=-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1},

(435)

and

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}=-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}.

(436)

From Eqs. (
360
),
(

), (
387
),
(
388
), and
(
430
),
we can see that the only matrix elements of Eq. (
430
)
that we need to calculate explicitly are
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}
,

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}
,
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}
,
and
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}
. First, we calculate

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}
. Using Eqs. (
370
),
(
400
),
(
428
), (
429
),
and (
431
) we
get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1},1}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1},0}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;0}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{0,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{2},1}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(437)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}
.
Using Eqs. (

),
(

),
(

), (

),

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1},1}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;0}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{2},1}\delta_{q_{1},0}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=0}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{=}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(438)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}
.
Using Eqs. (

),
(

), and (

)

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(439)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}
.
Using Eqs. (

),
(

), and
(
