) we get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left[\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(440)

Next, we calculate
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}
. Using Eqs. (

),
(

),
and (

)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,0}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}\\
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,1}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}&\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\end{pmatrix},

(441)

with

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}=\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1},

(442)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,0}=\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0},

(443)

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1},

(444)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,1}=\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1},

(445)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0}=\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,0},

(446)

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}.

(447)

From Eqs. (

),
(

), (

),
(

), and
(

),
we can see that the only matrix elements of Eq. (

,

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}

(

we get

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left[\delta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right).

(448)

\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;0}+\Theta_{q_{1},1}\delta_{0,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;0}\prod_{q=1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}-1}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\Theta_{q_{2},1}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{0,q_{2}}\prod_{q=0}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}-1}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\Theta_{q_{2},1}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}-1}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left(\quad\mathord{+}\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\Theta_{q_{2},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

\displaystyle\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1},

(455)

(457)

(459)

(460)

and

),

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\sum_{q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{3}}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,1}\right]_{q_{3},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\Theta_{q_{1},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}\delta_{q_{1},0}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{2},1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;0}+\delta_{0,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,1}\prod_{q=0}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\Theta_{q_{1},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}\Theta_{q_{1},1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,0}\delta_{q_{1},0}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=0}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right).

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;0}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}^{2}+\delta_{q_{1},0}\Theta_{q_{2}-1,1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=0}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},0}\delta_{0,q_{2}}+\delta_{q_{1},0}\Theta_{q_{2}-1,0}\prod_{q=0}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{g_{\nu;\epsilon}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=}\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{1}}+\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{=\mathord{+}\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left.\quad\mathord{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right).

(465)

We have now calculated the block elements of
\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}

that are required to calculate the right-hand-side of Eq. (
379
).
To summarize, these elements are:

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right),

(466)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right),

(467)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}},

(468)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{2},q_{1}}^{*},

(469)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right),

(470)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}\prod_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{2},q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right),

(471)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}},

(472)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{2},q_{1}}^{*},

(473)

\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right),

(474)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{2},q_{1}+1}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}+1}\prod_{q=q_{1}+1}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right),

(475)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}},

(476)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\right]_{q_{2},q_{1}}^{*},

(477)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right),

(478)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\frac{1}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}\prod_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\prod_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right),

(479)

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=g_{\nu;\epsilon}\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}},

(480)

and

\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,-1}\right]_{q_{1},q_{2}}=\left[\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,1}\right]_{q_{2},q_{1}}^{*}.

(481)

The next task is to calculate
\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right]
,
which appears on the right-hand-side of Eq. (
379
).
This is done in several steps. First, using Eqs. (
362
),
(
380
), and (
381
)
we can write

\displaystyle\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right]

\displaystyle=\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right]\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}-\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1}\right]

\displaystyle=\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right]\det\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\right].

(482)

Next, using Eqs. (
380
), (
381
),
and (
394
)
we can write

\displaystyle\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\det\left[\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+3\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}\right]\det\left[\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+2\right)}-\mathbf{0}_{\left(2n+2\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\left(2n+3\right)\times\left(2n+3\right)}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;0,1}^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=1.

(483)

Next, using Eqs. (
373
),
(
380
), (
381
),
(
406
), (
407
),
and (
424
)
we can write

\displaystyle\det\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\det\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]\det\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}-\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\det\left[\mathbf{B}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\right]\det\left[\left(\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\right)^{-1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\det\left[\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}-\delta_{q_{1}+1,q_{2}}f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q_{2}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\delta_{q_{1},2n+1}\delta_{0,q_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=1+\left(-1\right)^{2n+1}\left(-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;q}\right\}\right)\left(\prod_{q=1}^{2n+1}\left\{-f_{\nu;\epsilon;n;-1;q}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}.

(484)

Therefore, from Eqs. (
482
)-(
484
)
we have

\det\left[\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right]=1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}=1-e^{-\beta\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}.

(485)

The next task is to calculate
\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\psi}}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}
,
which appears on the right-hand-side of Eq. (
379
).
Using Eqs. (
354
)-(
356
),
(
360
),
(
361
), (
387
),
(
388
), (
411
),
(
430
),
(
441
),
and (
452
)
we get

\displaystyle\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\psi}}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}

\displaystyle=-\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;-1,-1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\tilde{\Gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right),

(486)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1},

(487)

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;-1,-1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1},

(488)

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\Gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;1}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}.

(489)

Towards the goal of calculating
\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\psi}}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}
,
we calculate each individual term on the right-hand-side of Eq. (
486
)
separately. Starting with the first term: using Eqs. (
357
),
(
373
), (
466
),
(
470
), (
474
),
(
478
), and (
487
)
we get

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}-\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}g_{\nu;\epsilon}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

(490)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(2\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\left[g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{+}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right]

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\left[g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\left\{1+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\right\}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\Theta_{q_{1}-2,q_{2}}\left[e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\left[2-g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-2,q_{2}}\left[g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-2,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathord{-}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-2,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{-}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-2,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{+}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right]

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\left[g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-\left\{1+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{-}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{+}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\left.\quad\mathop{+}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right]\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\left[1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\right]+\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\left[1+g_{\nu;\epsilon}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}\left[g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{-}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{+}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}\quad\mathop{+}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}}\left.\left.\quad\mathop{-}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right]\right),\hskip 56.9055pt

(491)

which we can write more concisely as

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}+

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\eta_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}+\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},

(492)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\left[-g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left.\quad\mathord{+}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left.\quad\mathord{+}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}}\left.\quad\mathord{-}g_{\nu;\epsilon}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right],

(493)

and as a reminder we use the convention

\sum_{q=a}^{b}F_{q}=\begin{cases}0,&\text{if }a>b,\\
F_{a}+F_{a+1}+\ldots+F_{b}&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

(494)

with
F_{q}
being an arbitrary sequence. Next, we calculate

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;-1,-1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;-1,-1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,1}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;-1}

\displaystyle\quad=\left\{\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,0;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0,1;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,0;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;0;-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right)^{\dagger}\mathbf{C}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,1;1,1}\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;\epsilon;n;1;-1}\right\}^{*}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}+\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{*}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}.

\displaystyle\quad=\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}.

\displaystyle\quad=\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+\Theta_{q_{2}-1,q_{1}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{1}}^{q_{2}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(g_{\nu;\epsilon}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad=\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{-}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}.

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(g_{\nu;\epsilon}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\tilde{\Gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;-1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,-1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right),

(500)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;-1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}-\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},

(501)

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,-1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}}\mathop{\times}\left(\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}-\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}g_{\nu;\epsilon}\delta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}}-g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1}-1,q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\mathrel{=}-\frac{\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}}{1-g_{\nu;\epsilon}}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{+}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}+\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+g_{\nu;\epsilon}\Theta_{q_{1},q_{2}+1}e^{-i\Delta t\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\left(\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right)^{2}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\delta_{q_{1},q_{2}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}.

(502)

By comparing the expressions in Eqs. (
501
)
and (
502
)
to that in Eq. (
490
),
and using Eq. (

),
one should be able to see that

\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;-1,1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=-\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},

(503)

and

\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;1,-1}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=-\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{q_{1}}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{*}\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}.

(504)

Next, using Eqs. (
486
), (

)-(
500
),
(
503
),
and (
504
)
we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\epsilon}\left(\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\boldsymbol{\psi}}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n}

\displaystyle\quad=-2\sum_{\epsilon}\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}-\sum_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{q_{1}=0}^{q_{2}}\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right),

(505)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathcal{E}_{\nu;r;q_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{\nu;r;q_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}-\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\left\{\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}-\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\right)\text{Re}\left[\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;n;q_{2},q_{1}}\right]+i\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}+\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\right)\text{Im}\left[\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;n;q_{2},q_{1}}\right]\right\},

(506)

and

\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}=\sum_{\epsilon}\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;\epsilon;n;q_{1},q_{2}}.

(507)

Next, we derive explicit expressions for
\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}

for different values of
q_{1}
, and
q_{2}
. We do this by making
use of the spectral density of the noise coupled to the
\nu
-component
of the spin at site
r
and temperature
T
,
A_{\nu;r;T}\left(\omega\right)
.
We define
A_{\nu;r;T}\left(\omega\right)
in Sec.
III.4

and also derive the following expression for the function therein:

A_{\nu;r;T}\left(\omega\right)=\text{sign}\left(\omega\right)\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\left|\omega\right|\right)}{1-e^{-\beta\omega}},

(508)

where

\text{sign}\left(\omega\right)=\begin{cases}1&\text{if }\omega>0,\\
-1&\text{if }\omega<0,\end{cases}

(509)

and
A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)
is the zero-temperature
limit of
A_{\nu;r;T}\left(\omega\right)
, which we calculate in
Sec.
III.4
to be:

A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)=2\pi\sum_{\epsilon}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{2}\delta\left(\omega-\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}\right),

(510)

before taking the continuum limit. Note that

\sum_{\epsilon}\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}^{2}f\left(\omega_{\epsilon}\right)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)f\left(\omega\right).

(511)

where
f\left(\omega\right)
is an arbitrary function.
Using Eq. (
368
), (
493
),
(
507
), and (
511
)
we get

\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;n;k_{1},k_{2}}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;T=0}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{-\beta\omega}\right\}}\left\{\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;k_{1},k_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)+e^{-\beta\omega}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;k_{1},k_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(-\omega\right)\right\},

(512)

where

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=k_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}.

(513)

Using Eq. (
510
),
we can rewrite Eq. (
512
)
as

\displaystyle\tilde{\eta}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{-\beta\omega}\right\}}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)+\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{-\beta\omega}\right\}}e^{-\beta\omega}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(-\omega\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{-\beta\omega}\right\}}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)+\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(-\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{\beta\omega}\right\}}e^{\beta\omega}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{-\beta\omega}\right\}}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)-\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(-\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}\left\{1-e^{-\beta\omega}\right\}}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{1}{\omega^{2}}\left[\text{sign}\left(\omega\right)\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\left|\omega\right|\right)}{1-e^{-\beta\omega}}\right]\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}\int_{-\infty}^{0}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{1}{\omega^{2}}\left[\text{sign}\left(\omega\right)\frac{A_{\nu;r;T=0}\left(\left|\omega\right|\right)}{1-e^{-\beta\omega}}\right]\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,\frac{A_{\nu;r;T}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right).

(514)

Next, we simplify
\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)
.
Using Eq. (
592
) and (
513
),
and noting that
q_{1}\geq q_{2}
for all instances of
\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)

in the path integral expression for
\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)
,
we can write

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)=\begin{cases}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q=0\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }q_{1}-q_{2}=0,\\
\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q=1\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }q_{1}-q_{2}=1,\\
\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q\geq 2\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }q_{1}-q_{2}\geq 2,\end{cases}

(515)

where

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q=0\right)}\left(\omega\right)=1-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}},

(516)

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q=1\right)}\left(\omega\right)=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}}-1-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}},

(517)

and

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q\geq 2\right)}\left(\omega\right)=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}.

(518)

Noting that

4\sin\left(a\right)\sin\left(b\right)=4\left\{\frac{e^{ia}-e^{-ia}}{2i}\right\}\left\{\frac{e^{ib}-e^{-ib}}{2i}\right\}=e^{i\left\{a-b\right\}}+e^{-i\left\{a-b\right\}}-e^{i\left\{a+b\right\}}-e^{-i\left\{a+b\right\}},

(519)

we can rewrite Eq. (
518
)
as follows:

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q\geq 2\right)}\left(\omega\right)

\displaystyle=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}}\tilde{w}_{n;q}}

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\left[e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}+e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}-e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle=4e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}{2}\right)\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}}{2}\right).

(520)

Moreover, using Eq. (
592
),
we can rewrite similarly Eq. (
517
)
as follows:

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q=1\right)}\left(\omega\right)

\displaystyle=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}}-1-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}+e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{-}e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{+}e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle=e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\left[e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}+e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}-\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{\mathrel{=}\mathop{\times}}\left.\quad\mathop{-}e^{\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}-e^{-\frac{i}{2}\Delta t\omega\left\{\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle=4e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}{2}\right)\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}}{2}\right).

(521)

Therefore, using Eqs. (
515
),
(
516
),
(
520
),
and (
521
)
we can express
\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)

as

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)=\begin{cases}\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q=0\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }q_{1}-q_{2}=0,\\
\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q\geq 1\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }q_{1}-q_{2}\geq 1,\end{cases}

(522)

where

\tilde{f}_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta q\geq 1\right)}\left(\omega\right)=4e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}+\sum_{q=q_{2}+1}^{q_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;q}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}\right\}}\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{1}}}{2}\right)\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;q_{2}}}{2}\right).

(523)

Now we are ready to calculate the bosonic integral in Eq. (
343
)
in its entirety. Using Eqs. (
23
),
(
345
)-(
348
),
(
379
), (
485
),
and (
505
) we get

\displaystyle\int\mathcal{D}b\,F_{n\vphantom{\nu;r;\epsilon}}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]};b\right)

\displaystyle\quad=e^{-2\sum_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\sum_{q=0}^{2n+1}\sum_{u,r,\epsilon}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{v;r;q}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\frac{\lambda_{\nu;r;\epsilon}}{\omega_{\nu;\epsilon}}\right\}^{2}}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\left\{\mathcal{Z}_{u;y}^{\left(B\right)}\mathcal{Z}_{u;z}^{\left(B\right)}\right\}\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\nu;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\right\},

(524)

where

\tilde{I}_{\nu;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\nu;r+uL;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r+uL;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\right\},

(525)

with

\tilde{I}_{\nu;r;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)=\prod_{q_{2}=0}^{2n+1}\prod_{q_{1}=0}^{q_{2}}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)\right\},

(526)

and

\tilde{I}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)=e^{-\tilde{\gamma}_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1,q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1,q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1,q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1,q_{2}}\right)}.

(527)

Using Eqs. (
343
)
and (
524
), we can rewrite
the path integral expression for
\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)

as

\displaystyle\rho^{\left(A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;2n+1},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;2n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\equiv\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;2n+1}z\right|\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;2n+1}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left[\prod_{q=0}^{2n+1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{y;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\prod_{q=0}^{2n}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\prod_{l=1}^{n}\left\{\delta_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;2l-1},\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;2l}}\right\}e^{i\sum_{\alpha=\pm 1}\tilde{\phi}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{y;\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\tilde{I}_{\alpha;n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\right\}\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\left\{\tilde{I}_{\nu;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,2n+1\right]}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\rho^{\left(i,A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right].

(528)

Next, we apply the
\delta_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;2l-1},\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;2l}}

in Eq. (
528
) to reduce
the number of
\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}
-degrees of freedom
by roughly half and make the following symbol substitutions:

\displaystyle\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q=2l}

\displaystyle\to\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q=l},\quad\text{for }0\leq l\leq n,

(529)

\displaystyle\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q=2n+1}

\displaystyle\to\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q=n+1}.

(530)

Upon doing so, Eq. (
528
)
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\rho^{\left(A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;n+1},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\equiv\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;n+1}z\right|\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;n+1}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad=\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left[\prod_{q=0}^{\tilde{q}_{y;n}}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{y;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\prod_{q=0}^{\tilde{q}_{z;n}-1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\right]e^{i\sum_{\alpha=\pm 1}\phi_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{z;n}\right]}\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}I_{n}^{\left(\text{yz-noise}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)\rho^{\left(i,A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right],

(531)

where

\phi_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{z;n}\right]}\right)=\sum_{q=0}^{\tilde{q}_{z;n}}\phi_{\alpha;n;k=q}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right),

(532)

with

\phi_{\alpha;n;k}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right)=-\frac{\alpha\Delta t}{2}\sum_{k^{\prime}=k-1}^{k}w_{n;k^{\prime}}H_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k^{\prime}};\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right),

(533)

H_{z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k^{\prime}};\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right)=\sum_{u=-N}^{N}H_{u;z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{k^{\prime}};\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right),

(534)

H_{u;z}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t;\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q}\right)=\sum_{r=0}^{L-1}h_{z;r}\left(t\right)\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}+\sum_{r=1}^{L-1}J_{z,z;r,r+1}\left(t\right)\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}\sigma_{z;r+uL+1;\alpha;q},

(535)

w_{n;k}=\begin{cases}0,&\text{if }k=-1,n+1,\\
\frac{1}{2},&\text{if }k=0,n,\\
1,&\text{if }0<k<n;\end{cases}

(536)

I_{n}^{\left(\text{yz-noise}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)

is the total influence:

I_{n}^{\left(\text{yz-noise}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)=\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left\{I_{r+uL;n}^{\left(\text{yz-noise}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r+uL;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)\right\},

(537)

with
I_{r;n}^{\left(\text{yz-noise}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)

being the influence functional that couples the Ising spin variables

\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}

with one another [including Ising spin variables associated with
different times]:

\displaystyle I_{r;n}^{\left(\text{yz-noise}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=I_{n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)\mathop{\times}I_{y;r;n}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{y;n}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\prod_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\}}\left\{I_{\nu;r;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)\right\},

(538)

\displaystyle I_{n}^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=\prod_{l=0}^{n}\left\{I^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z,1\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1,2l+1},\sigma_{y;r;-1,2l+1},\sigma_{z;r;1,l+1},\sigma_{z;r;-1,l+1}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\phantom{=\prod_{l=0}^{n}}\left.\quad\mathop{\times}I^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z,2\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1,2l},\sigma_{y;r;-1,2l},\sigma_{z;r;1,l},\sigma_{z;r;-1,l}\right)\right\},

(539)

\displaystyle I^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z,1\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1,2l+1},\sigma_{y;r;-1,2l+1},\sigma_{z;r;1,l+1},\sigma_{z;r;-1,l+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=I^{\left(y\to z\right)}\left(\sigma_{z;r;1,l+1},\sigma_{y;r;1,2l+1}\right)I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;-1,2l+1},\sigma_{z;r;-1,l+1}\right),

(540)

\displaystyle I^{\left(y\leftrightarrow z,2\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1,2l},\sigma_{y;r;-1,2l},\sigma_{z;r;1,l},\sigma_{z;r;-1,l}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1,2l},\sigma_{z;r;1,l}\right)I^{\left(y\to z\right)}\left(\sigma_{z;r;-1,l},\sigma_{y;r;-1,2l}\right),

(541)

\displaystyle I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}},\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left.\left\langle\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}y\right|\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}z\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\left\{1-i\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}+\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}+i\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right\},

(542)

\displaystyle I^{\left(y\to z\right)}\left(\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}},\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left.\left\langle\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}z\right|\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}}y\right\rangle=\left\{I^{\left(z\to y\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q^{\vphantom{\prime}}},\sigma_{z;r;\alpha;q^{\prime}}\right)\right\}^{*},

(543)

I_{y;r;n}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{y;n}\right]}\right)=\prod_{l=0}^{n}\left\{I_{y;r;n;k=l}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1;2l},\sigma_{y;r;-1;2l},\sigma_{y;r;1;2l+1},\sigma_{y;r;-1;2l+1}\right)\right\},

(544)

I_{y;r;n;k}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{y;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{y;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{y;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)=I_{y;r;1;n;k}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{y;r;1;q_{1}}\right)I_{y;r;-1;n;k}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{y;r;-1;q_{2}}\right),

(545)

I_{y;r;\alpha;n;k}^{\left(\text{tfc}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q_{1}},\sigma_{y;r;\alpha;q_{2}}\right)=\begin{cases}\left\langle\sigma_{y;r;-1;q_{1}}y\right|e^{i\Delta tw_{n;k}h_{x;r}\left(t_{k}\right)\hat{\sigma}_{x;r}}\left|\sigma_{y;r;-1;q_{2}}y\right\rangle,&\text{if }\alpha=-1,\\
\left\langle\sigma_{y;r;1;q_{2}}y\right|e^{-i\Delta tw_{n;k}h_{x;r}\left(t_{k}\right)\hat{\sigma}_{x;r}}\left|\sigma_{y;r;1;q_{1}}y\right\rangle,&\text{if }\alpha=1,\end{cases}

(546)

I_{\nu;r;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)=\prod_{q_{2}=0}^{\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}}\prod_{q_{1}=0}^{q_{2}}\left\{I_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)\right\},

(547)

\displaystyle I_{\nu;r;n;q_{1},q_{2}}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\prod_{\left(l_{1},l_{2}\right)\in\Upsilon_{\nu;n;q_{1},q_{2}}}\left\{e^{-\gamma_{\nu;r;n;l_{1},l_{2}}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)}\right\},

(548)

\Upsilon_{y;n;q_{1},q_{2}}=\left\{\left(q_{1},q_{2}\right)\right\},

(549)

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{z;n;q_{1},q_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=\begin{cases}\left\{\left(0,0\right)\right\},&\text{if }q_{1}=q_{2}=0,\\
\left\{\left(0,2q_{2}-1\right),\left(0,2q_{2}\right)\right\},&\text{if }q_{1}=0\text{ and }1\leq q_{2}\leq n,\\
\left\{\left(0,2n+1\right)\right\},&\text{if }q_{1}=0\text{ and }q_{2}=n+1,\\
\left\{\left(2q_{1}-1,2q_{2}-1\right),\left(2q_{1},2q_{2}-1\right),\left(2q_{1}-1,2q_{2}\right),\left(2q_{1},2q_{2}\right)\right\},&\text{if }1\leq q_{1}<q_{2}\leq n,\\
\left\{\left(2q_{1}-1,2q_{2}-1\right),\left(2q_{1}-1,2q_{2}\right),\left(2q_{1},2q_{2}\right)\right\},&\text{if }1\leq q_{1}=q_{2}\leq n,\\
\left\{\left(2q_{1}-1,2n+1\right),\left(2q_{1},2n+1\right)\right\},&\text{if }1\leq q_{1}\leq n\text{ and }q_{2}=n+1,\\
\left\{\left(2n+1,2n+1\right)\right\},&\text{if }q_{1}=q_{2}=n+1,\end{cases}

(550)

\displaystyle\gamma_{\nu;r;n;l_{1},l_{2}}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}},\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}},\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad=\mathcal{E}_{\nu;r;l_{1}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\mathcal{E}_{\nu;r;l_{2}}^{\left(\lambda\right)}\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{2}}-\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\left\{\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}-\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\right)\text{Re}\left[\eta_{\nu;r;n;l_{2},l_{1}}\right]+i\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}+\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\right)\text{Im}\left[\eta_{\nu;r;n;l_{2},l_{1}}\right]\right\},

(551)

\eta_{\nu;r;n;l_{1},l_{2}}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{d\omega}{2\pi}\,A_{\nu;r;T}\left(\omega\right)\frac{f_{n;l_{1},l_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)}{\omega^{2}},

(552)

f_{n;l_{1},l_{2}}^{\left(\eta\right)}\left(\omega\right)=\begin{cases}f_{n;l_{1}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta l=0\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }l_{1}-l_{2}=0,\\
f_{n;l_{1},l_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta l\geq 1\right)}\left(\omega\right),&\text{if }l_{1}-l_{2}\geq 1,\end{cases}

(553)

f_{n;l_{1}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta l=0\right)}\left(\omega\right)=\text{Re}\left[1-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;l_{1}}}\right]=2\sin^{2}\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;l_{1}}}{2}\right),

(554)

f_{n;l_{1},l_{2}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta l\geq 1\right)}\left(\omega\right)=4e^{-i\Delta t\omega\left\{\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;l_{1}}+\sum_{l=l_{2}+1}^{l_{1}-1}\tilde{w}_{n;l}+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{w}_{n;l_{2}}\right\}}\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;l_{1}}}{2}\right)\sin\left(\frac{\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;l_{2}}}{2}\right),

(555)

\tilde{w}_{n;l}=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{4},&\text{if }l=0,1,2n,2n+1,\\
\frac{1}{2},&\text{if }1<l<2n.\end{cases}

(556)

Note that one could set
f_{n;l_{1}}^{\left(\eta,\Delta l=0\right)}\left(\omega\right)

to
1-e^{-i\Delta t\omega\tilde{w}_{n;l_{1}}}
in Eq. (
554
)
based on the discussions leading to Eq. (
516
),
however only the real part is ever used as should be clear from Eq. (
551
)
and the fact that

\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}-\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\right)\left(\sigma_{\nu;r;1;q_{1}}+\sigma_{\nu;r;-1;q_{1}}\right)=0.

(557)

All that remains is to show how the path integral expression for
\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)

in Eq. (
531
) simplifies
when there is no coupling between the environment and the
y
-components
of the spins, i.e. when
A_{y;r;T}\left(\omega\right)=0
. Noting
that

I_{y;r;n}^{\left(\text{bath}\right)}\left(\sigma_{y;r;\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{y;n}\right]}\right)=1,\quad\text{if }A_{y;r;T}\left(\omega\right)=0,

(558)

we can rewrite Eq. (
531
)
as

\displaystyle\rho^{\left(A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;n+1},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;n+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;n+1}z\right|\hat{\rho}^{\left(A\right)}\left(t_{n}\right)\left|\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;n+1}z\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\prod_{\alpha=\pm 1}\prod_{u=-N}^{N}\prod_{r=0}^{L-1}\left[\prod_{q=0}^{\tilde{q}_{z;n}-1}\left\{\sum_{\sigma_{z;r+uL;\alpha;q}=\pm 1}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}e^{i\sum_{\alpha=\pm 1}\phi_{\alpha;n}^{\left(\text{lcafc}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;\alpha;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{z;n}\right]}\right)}I_{n}^{\left(\text{z-noise}\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{\nu\in\left\{y,z\right\};\alpha=\pm 1;q\in\left[0,\tilde{q}_{\nu;n}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle\mathrel{\phantom{=}}\mathop{\times}\rho^{\left(i,A\right)}\left(\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;1;0},\boldsymbol{\sigma}_{z;-1;0}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left[\Delta t^{2}\right],

(559)

where
